## com.jaymospowers.power.chrono.mixin (main)
- `ChronoBlockItemMixin` [23] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/chrono/mixin/ChronoBlockItemMixin.java`: Remembers which blocks a Chrono player places (their echoes place the favourite one).
- `ChronoHeldPhaseMixin` [38] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/chrono/mixin/ChronoHeldPhaseMixin.java`: A player holding Overclock's phase (ChronoPhase#phasing) is stopped by no block but the floor under its feet and the blocks nothing can break (bedrock, barriers).
- `ChronoLivingEntityMixin` [25] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/chrono/mixin/ChronoLivingEntityMixin.java`: Stasis Field: a frozen mob skips aiStep entirely (AI, jumping, travel and gravity), but still ticks otherwise, so it can be hurt and killed.
- `ChronoMobStunMixin` [29] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/chrono/mixin/ChronoMobStunMixin.java`: Time Flow's stun: a stunned mob thinks nothing (no goals, no path, no move control) and its own walking stops, but it still falls and can still be thrown.
- `ChronoRapidBowMixin` [21] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/chrono/mixin/ChronoRapidBowMixin.java`: A speedster's bow is at full draw after a few ticks: released then, the arrow goes as if drawn for the whole time.
- `ChronoRapidCrossbowMixin` [22] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/chrono/mixin/ChronoRapidCrossbowMixin.java`: A speedster's crossbow loads at once: its charge takes a tick.
- `ChronoRespawnMixin` [31] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/chrono/mixin/ChronoRespawnMixin.java`: Respawn clicked on the death screen while a Chrono's death is held back (ChronoCheatDeath): the death goes ahead first, so the respawn that follows is an ordinary one.
- `ChronoRunBlowMixin` [21] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/chrono/mixin/ChronoRunBlowMixin.java`: A melee blow landed at a sprint by a speedster hits harder the faster it goes (see ChronoRapid#blowBonus).
- `ChronoServerPlayerAccessor` (interface) [13] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/chrono/mixin/ChronoServerPlayerAccessor.java`: Chrono's held-back death: the health last sent, so the real (held) health isn't sent over the death screen.
- `ChronoWaterRunMixin` [45] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/chrono/mixin/ChronoWaterRunMixin.java`: Overclock's water run: a sprinting, overclocked player stands on water the way a strider stands on lava (the surface of still water turns solid underfoot, on both sides).
- `FutureSunMixin` [20] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/chrono/mixin/FutureSunMixin.java`: Nothing burns under the future's ashen sun: its feral dead roam by day too.
- `SunBurnMixin` [38] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/chrono/mixin/SunBurnMixin.java`: Burn in daylight only when the sky is truly open to the Sun: a ray from any part of the mob's body toward the Sun's real position must pass unobstructed.

## com.jaymospowers.power.darktide.mixin (main)
- `DarktideAirMixin` [21] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/darktide/mixin/DarktideAirMixin.java`: A Darktide user never runs out of air (both sides, so no bubbles flicker on the HUD).
- `DarktideBlockMixin` [26] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/darktide/mixin/DarktideBlockMixin.java`: The Darktide guard takes its share of a hit where a shield would (so vanilla treats a parry as a full block: no knockback, no hurt flash).
- `DarktideDeflectMixin` [27] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/darktide/mixin/DarktideDeflectMixin.java`: A guarding Darktide blade sends projectiles back at their shooter (see DarktideGuard#deflection).
- `DarktideDropMixin` [22] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/darktide/mixin/DarktideDropMixin.java`: Dying with Tide Arms out: the conjured blades go and the real items come back before the inventory spills.
- `DarktideMobStunMixin` [26] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/darktide/mixin/DarktideMobStunMixin.java`: Darktide's stun: a stunned mob thinks nothing and stops walking, but still falls and can still be shoved.
- `DarktidePlayerPoseMixin` [23] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/darktide/mixin/DarktidePlayerPoseMixin.java`: A crippled player crawls (both sides read the synced cripple, so the pose and hitbox agree).
- `DarktideRisenTargetMixin` [23] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/darktide/mixin/DarktideRisenTargetMixin.java`: A risen shadow never takes its master (or a fellow shadow) as its target, whatever its own AI wants.
- `DarktideVisibilityMixin` [32] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/darktide/mixin/DarktideVisibilityMixin.java`: Veil: mobs only notice a veiled Darktide user within the veil's range (their follow range is scaled down to it).
